\title{Melanie-MASTERS (human masters) — Scotch Game}

\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{Qe2}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{Qe2} (\texttt{d1e2}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0019\#3166} (ply 23) [structural distance $d$=65, QAP obj=23]: the master played \texttt{Qe7+}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Qd1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Qd1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{N}b1 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{p}a7 as their \emph{B}c8; transferred move \texttt{d1e2} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0003\#1205} (ply 20) [structural distance $d$=65, QAP obj=76]: the master played \texttt{c4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Rh1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pc2} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{N}b1 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{p}c6 as their \emph{B}c1; transferred move \texttt{h1g1}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0024\#1703} (ply 21) [structural distance $d$=68, QAP obj=30]: the master played \texttt{Rc8}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Nb1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ra1} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{B}f1 as their \emph{P}a7, our \emph{r}a8 as their \emph{B}b7; transferred move \texttt{b1c3}. Synthesis: 1 retrieved analog transferred to \texttt{Qe2} (summed QAP objective 23, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m12}
